Deploy script `scripts/deploy/deploy-nextjs.sh` also hardened across 5 commits (00926aea/152787e8/13c5d11c/12bb62bb/8ab85fa4): stop_node_on_port + backup/extract + start refactored to scp'd .ps1 files (no more inline multi-line PowerShell-over-SSH), EXPECTED_COMMIT computed from SCRIPT_DIR (kiosk/ has stray `.git/` scaffold that broke `git rev-parse`), freshness gate requires health `git_commit` match before claiming success, watchdog-wait start path (relies on FrontendWatchdog instead of custom Start-Process which loses env vars). **Evidence — Server (.23) :3300:** /kiosk/api/health returns `git_commit:"8ab85fa4"`, `build_id:"yVPrraPc9n4gdjDbQVfec"`; chrome-devtools MCP navigation of `http://192.168.31.23:3300/kiosk/pod/1` observed in-browser redirect → `http://192.168.31.23:3300/kiosk/staff?return=%2Fkiosk%2Fpod%2F1` (snapshot captured "STAFF TERMINAL" / "Tap to Sign In" a11y tree). **Evidence — Bono VPS :3300:** /kiosk/api/health returns `git_commit:"8ab85fa4"`, `build_id:"opx_9dmmPiCfAzgji9EQR"`; chrome-devtools navigation of `http://100.70.177.44:3300/kiosk/pod/1` same redirect behavior. **Ops discoveries fixed this session:** (a) FrontendWatchdog scheduled task `StartFrontendWatchdog` had last run 2026-04-17 12:08:05 with Last Result 1 — dead for 2 days — restarted via `schtasks /Run /TN StartFrontendWatchdog`, log now shows 17:40:34 monitoring kiosk/web/admin. (b) `$env` in PowerShell is a reserved automatic variable — using `$envVars = @{...}` in helper scripts is required. (c) ProcessStartInfo with `RedirectStandardOutput=true` + no drainer thread causes child node to die when parent PowerShell session ends (SSH disconnect) — swap ps1 did this once, recovered by rerunning schtasks watchdog. (d) UTF-8 em-dash (`—`) in .ps1 files uploaded via scp and parsed with Windows codepage causes misleading PS parser errors at unrelated lines — ASCII-only for ps1 content going to .23. (e) VPS kiosk `prebuild` script `node ../scripts/inject-git-commit.js` didn't run on its own during `npm run build` the first time (unclear why — possibly the validate-frontend-env.sh exit code gated it) — manual rerun wrote correct `public/git-commit.txt`. | 2026-04-18 22:41 IST | James | 2c27e2fc | **fix(kiosk-dead-ends): zombie half-socket + BILL-14 sim_type=None silent abort.** Two independent fixes for "kiosk not launching games / display not showing information": (1) `ws/dashboard_handler.rs` — the spawned send_task owned `rx` (broadcast receiver); when it died early (Lagged>500, 5s slow-send timeout, 45s pong timeout), rx was dropped and `dashboard_tx.receiver_count()` decremented but the outer receiver loop kept blocking on `receiver.next()` (WS is half-duplex). Result: DASHBOARD_CLIENT_COUNT=19 while real receiver_count trended to 0, every `dashboard_tx.send(GameStateChanged(info))` returned SendError("channel closed"), kiosk UI stopped updating until client happened to send. Fix: `tokio::select!` between send_task and recv_task — whichever exits first aborts the other, socket fully closes, client reconnects with fresh rx. (2) `billing_timer_expiry_timeout.rs` — INV-10's sim-default-guard refused to retry launch when sim_type=None and advanced attempt=2 for cancel-with-refund, but emitted NO kiosk event. Staff saw hung launch spinner until refund tick arrived. Fix: on `bail_no_sim`, broadcast `DashboardEvent::CommandError { command: "launch_game", error: "Launch failed — sim type unknown. Your credits will be refunded. Please try launching the game again." }` + log_pod_activity audit entry. Evidence today: 16:22:57 pod_1 AC timeout→16:25:24 BILL-14 sim None; 14:46:31 pod_4 AC ACK→14:49:32 BILL-14 sim None; 11:49 pod_4 F1 25 2/2 exhausted. | 2026-04-09 14:59 IST | James | d027332a | fix(ci): add RUSTFLAGS=-C target-feature=-crt-static to unblock rc-sentry-ai workspace build. Root cause: rc-sentry-ai depends on `ort 2.0.0-rc.12` with feature `cuda`; `ort-sys` compiles the entire ONNX Runtime C++ tree from source via cc-rs on windows-latest. Recent MSVC/ucrt toolchain drift exposed a CRT (/MD vs /MT) linkage mismatch — C++ TUs emit `__imp_<crt_fn>` dllimport references (tolower, fgetpos, _fstat64i32, _difftime64, _mktime64, llroundl, strtod, strtof, acoshf, asinhf, atanhf, nearbyintf, remainderf, _towupper_l, iswalpha, fmax, modf, _wchdir, _wmkdir, _waccess, _wstat64i32, _dup, _dup2, strncpy, frexp, etc.) but the linker was resolving `libucrt.lib` (static CRT) which doesn't export them with the `__imp_` prefix → `LNK1120: 33 unresolved externals` in rc_sentry_ai.exe. Fix: set `RUSTFLAGS=-C target-feature=-crt-static` at workflow env level — forces dynamic CRT linkage so `__imp_*` symbols resolve from ucrt.dll import lib. Matches the known-working local build recipe from memory/project_camera_architecture.md:15 (James has been building rc-sentry-ai locally with this flag). Initial approach (Path A) was to `--exclude rc-sentry-ai` from `cargo build --workspace` — committed locally as 2468bae8 then reset and replaced with this root-cause fix after memory check surfaced the RUSTFLAGS recipe. G9 for missing memory-first check at start of debug: `feedback_memory_first_always.md` already mandates grep over memory/ before debugging, self-enforcement only — no new rule. Evidence: gh run 24181655147 (failed), log lines 3494-3566 (LNK2001/LNK2019/LNK1120 errors). Scope: one env var addition to .github/workflows/ci.yml, no changes to Cargo.toml, no changes to rc-sentry-ai source. RUSTFLAGS applies to all cargo invocations in the build job but Rust's MSVC default is already dynamic CRT, so explicitly setting `-crt-static` should be a no-op for other workspace crates (rc-agent-crate, rc-sentry, rc-common, racecontrol, etc.) that currently build fine without the flag. NOT VERIFIED YET: CI run has not executed against this fix — verification pending next push-triggered run. Other parallel workflows (quality-gate.yml clippy, test/gate-check.sh, scripts/hooks/pre-push) untouched; they don't exhibit the issue. |

## Cause Elimination Template

Standing rule: any bug taking >30 min to isolate MUST use `bash scripts/fix_log.sh` before declaring fixed.

### Cause Elimination — EXAMPLE (2026-03-24)

**Symptom:** Pod healer declares lock screen DOWN on all 8 pods despite health endpoint returning 200. ForceRelaunchBrowser fires every 30s causing screen flicker.

**Hypotheses:**
- H1: Health endpoint not returning 200
- H2: curl output has unexpected formatting (quotes, whitespace)
- H3: u32::parse failing on valid-looking input
- H4: Pod healer threshold wrong (comparing against wrong status code)

**Elimination:**
- H1: tested curl -s http://pod:8090/health — returns "200" — ELIMINATED (endpoint works)
- H2: tested echo output — value is `"200"` with surrounding quotes — CONFIRMED (quotes break parse)
- H3: tested u32::parse("\"200\"") — fails, falls back to unwrap_or(0) — CONFIRMED (downstream of H2)
- H4: threshold is 200, comparison is correct — ELIMINATED

**Confirmed cause:** curl -w "%{http_code}" output wrapped in quotes by PowerShell $r variable. u32::parse("\"200\"") fails, unwrap_or(0) returns 0, healer thinks pod is down.

**Verification:** Deployed fix (strip quotes with tr -d '"'), verified curl output is now `200` (no quotes), u32::parse succeeds, healer reports HEALTHY on all 8 pods.

- Bono VPS: already ahead at `b1b53ee4` with binary `ca4f8ba6` built at 19:04 IST. `RC_IS_CLOUD=1 pm2 restart racecontrol --update-env` → `pm2 save` for reboot persistence. Verified `build_id=ca4f8ba6 status=ok`.
- Pods 1-8 rc-agent: `pod-deploy.sh d4b60fb5` (SCP method, HTTP bind blocked by sandbox). All 8 verified on `d4b60fb5` via :8090/health. POS unreachable (pre-existing SAC block).

### kiosk_lockdown_enabled flip
1. PUT venue `{kiosk_lockdown_enabled:"false"}` → 200. Broadcast to connected pods (WS).
2. Cloud_sync_pull (~51s cadence) overwrote venue's false with cloud's true; pods went lockdown-ENABLED again.
3. Direct SQL on Bono VPS cloud DB: `UPDATE kiosk_settings SET value='false' WHERE key='kiosk_lockdown_enabled'`.
4. Re-PUT venue → 200. After 30s stabilization, venue+cloud both `false`, no sync flap.
5. Pod 7 (worst at 91 events/3d): last LOCKDOWN 12:20:18Z, flip at 14:12Z, zero events after.

### deploy-server.sh pivot
- Prior "kill → verify dead → swap" pattern failed because an unidentified respawner launches racecontrol <3s after kill (NOT rc-server-sentry alone, NOT any of 9 RC schtasks, NOT Windows service — root cause open).
- Pivoted to swap-first-kill-second (same as pod-deploy.sh pattern). Windows allows renaming a running exe. Kill old process → watchdog restarts from NEW `racecontrol.exe`. Bypasses the kill-race.
- Committed script fix: `176734f` in deploy-staging repo.

### Evidence
- Server `build_id=d4b60fb5`, endpoints 200/401 (auth-gated).
- Bono VPS `build_id=ca4f8ba6`, `RC_IS_CLOUD=1` in `pm2 env 10`.
- All 8 pods `build_id=d4b60fb5` via :8090/health.
- `kiosk_lockdown_enabled=false` on both venue (API) and cloud (SQL).
- Pod 2 rc-agent jsonl: `Kiosk lockdown DISABLED via remote settings` at 14:23:54Z.

## 2026-04-18 21:10 IST — LOCKDOWN guard removed (code-level)

### Action
Short-circuited the rc-agent periodic process-whitelist scan branch in
`crates/rc-agent/src/event_loop.rs` with `if false && ...`. Previously
`kiosk_lockdown_enabled=false` (remote setting) disabled the *effect*
(kiosk stays inactive → `should_enforce()` returns false). This code
change disables the *scan* itself at the branch level, so even if the
flag is flipped to true later, no periodic process scan fires and no
LOCKDOWN event is emitted from this path. `CoreToAgentMessage::SettingsUpdated`
handler in `ws_handler.rs` still responds to the remote setting (kiosk
activate/deactivate UI), but the allowlist enforcement is dead.

### Why
Fleet-wide coordinated iRacingService64.exe crashes (Event 7034 "terminated
unexpectedly") repeating on all 7 pods within seconds of each other
at specific times across 4-14 → 4-17 — exact correlation not proven to
LOCKDOWN guard (the guard doesn't kill processes; it only triggers a UI
overlay), but the allowlist-maintenance burden (hashed/versioned Windows
Update binaries, EA anti-cheat churn, Garage61 hashed names, Steam
helpers) made the signal-to-noise ratio of the guard negative in practice.
462 LOCKDOWN events over 3 days with zero verified malware-blocks.

### Files
- `crates/rc-agent/src/event_loop.rs` (+11/-1): wrapped branch with `if false &&`

### NOT tested
- Whether iRacingService fleet crashes stop after deploy (observation
  window needed, will be visible over next 24-48h).
- Whether remote `kiosk_lockdown_enabled=true` still renders the UI
  overlay (SettingsUpdated handler still wired, but the scan-driven
  LOCKDOWN flag set is now dead code — user-facing effect of remote flag
  is limited to `state.kiosk.activate()` / `deactivate()`).
- POS `.130` (SAC-blocked, pre-existing, rc-agent not running there).


## 2026-04-18 21:25 IST — iRacingService fleet-wide auto-recovery + pod-provisioning fix

### Problem
iRacingService (iRacing.com Helper Service, `iRacingService64.exe`) was
crashing fleet-wide ~daily for weeks. 9+ Event 7034 ("terminated
unexpectedly") per pod across 14-04 → 18-04, tightly correlated across
pods (<60s spread between pod crashes on same event). No failure-recovery
config → service stayed STOPPED until manually started or next reboot.
When stopped, iRacing launches fail ~60s later with "Steam dialog
visible, no game window" (customer impact: Vishal on Pod 4 at 14:46Z today).

### Investigation
Cross-pod enumeration: 6/7 pods on venue had iRacingService STOPPED,
Pod 8 doesn't have it installed. Crash-cause research: Application log
has no Application Error events for iRacingService (service exits
voluntarily, not SEGV); WER queue empty; no iRacingService provider
events. Crash timing tightly correlated with rc-agent restart cycles
(21:08 deploy → 7 pods crashed at 21:08:16-21:09:10 within 54s).

Kill-list audit in rc-agent source: `enforce_safe_state` in
ac_launcher.rs had `iRacingService.exe` in its game_processes kill list,
but `taskkill /IM iRacingService.exe` does NOT match the actual binary
`iRacingService64.exe` (verified by direct ssh test: "ERROR: process
not found"). Removed the stale name anyway for correctness.

### Fix (this commit)
`scripts/install-pod-service.sh` step 5 — after setting up RCSentry,
configure iRacingService auto-recovery (if present):

  sc failure iRacingService reset= 86400 actions= restart/60000/restart/60000/restart/60000

This means: crashed iRacingService auto-restarts within 60s (up to 3
attempts), counter resets every 24h. Reimaged pods will pick this up
on next install-pod-service.sh run. Existing pods already configured
manually this session via direct sc.exe failure calls (all 7 pods
with iRacingService installed).

### NOT fixed
- Root cause of iRacingService voluntary exit on rc-agent restart. The
  rc-agent source doesn't directly kill iRacingService64.exe by name;
  something indirect (display/session/RPC/Steam state) causes self-exit.
  Passive 6-min observation window shows service stays UP when rc-agent
  isn't being restarted, so the trigger is real but not yet isolated.
- Pod 8 has no iRacingService installed (service absent from `sc query`).
  iRacing will not launch there. Requires full iRacing install via Steam.

### Files
- `scripts/install-pod-service.sh` (+10 lines, step 5)
- `crates/rc-agent/src/ac_launcher.rs` (prior commit `b39c2a6f`, removed
  iRacingService.exe from kill list + added iRacingSim64DX12.exe)

made `compute_session_cost` ignore its `_tiers` parameter and use

  90000 (overflow caps at pkg_60 via `cost.min(pkg_60_price)` — customer
  gets best deal), 90 min → 135000 (pkg_60 + 30 × overflow_rate 1500).
  Added comment noting tiers arg is ignored since 29dd79a8.

  The "soft-deleting a tier excludes it from cost" behavior is
  structurally dead once compute_session_cost stopped reading tiers;
  preserved for future pricing-model refactor reference.

- Pre-commit security gate: passed. Smart Pipe SAST + secrets + deps:

  not to assert correctness of the behavior itself.

## 2026-04-19 07:35 IST — Pattern I fix-forward: venue + pod wave to 66fec05c

### Problem
Pod 1 + Pod 6 rc-agent had been WS-disconnected from server since ~2026-04-18 23:05 IST
(Pattern I class — silent-reconnect-forever). Both pods' rc-agent alive and HTTP-reachable
throughout (4h+ uptime), but the WS reconnect loop kept the connection dead because the
90s liveness guard fired false-positives during idle periods. Server was degraded
(FLEET_PARTIAL 7/9) for ~8 hours.

### Fix (deployed this session)
Pattern I defense-in-depth (both commits already on origin/main and cloud; absent from venue):
- `09acbbe4` — rc-agent sends WS-level Message::Ping on every heartbeat_interval tick (5s).
  Server tungstenite auto-Pongs, which lands as Ok(_) in ws_rx and refreshes
  `last_server_frame_at` — preventing idle false-positive reconnect storms.
- `90b04d71` — racecontrol clones `agent_senders` entry BEFORE the `.await` on 12 dispatch
  sites. Removes the lock-across-await anti-pattern that could cause sender starvation.

### Deploy sequence
1. Venue racecontrol `.23`: `deploy-server.sh` with SENTRY_KEY (user provided via
   `/d/racecontrol.toml` pre-flight hook; key value never echoed to assistant stdout).
   Swapped `84a8b69a` → `66fec05c` at ~07:29 IST. Cloud auto-parity leg re-ran on Bono
   VPS (already at 66fec05c, rebuild was no-op functionally).
2. Pod wave via rc-sentry `:8091/exec`. Server sentry_service_key confirmed shared with
   pod rc-sentry (auth worked fleet-wide). For each pod: download from James:18889
   staging → size-verify (26,736,128 bytes match) → atomic swap (taskkill + del prev
   + ren current→prev + ren new→current) → 13s wait → `:8090/health` build_id verify.
3. Order: Pod 8 canary → Pods 2, 3, 4, 5, 7 → Pods 1, 6 last. POS skipped (no
   rc-sentry running, not Pattern I affected).

### Verified
- **BEHAVIOR:** WS connectivity of 8 pods + POS to venue racecontrol after binary swap.
  Pod 1 and Pod 6 specifically transitioning from `ws=False, build=e7e01ae3` (stuck 4h+)
  to `ws=True, build=66fec05c`.
- **RAW OUTPUT:** `/api/v1/health` returned `build_id=66fec05c, status=ok,
  fleet_connectivity: 9/9 pods connected (None)`. `/api/v1/fleet/health` showed all 9
  entries `ws=True, build=66fec05c` (except POS still on `e7e01ae3`).
- **WHERE:** James `.27` → LAN curl to `.23:8080` and direct to each pod `:8090/health`
  and `:8091/exec`.

### NOT tested
- **30-min idle soak.** Pattern I is an idle-window false-positive; fix is DEFENSIVE
  against a failure mode that might not re-fire for hours. Clean fleet at T+30s is
  necessary but not sufficient. Real verification: Pod 1+6 stay WS=True through next
  full idle period (30+ min no server-originated traffic). Schedule re-probe.
- **Wire-level Ping-Pong observation.** The `09acbbe4` mechanism adds a
  `Message::Ping(Vec::new())` every 5s. Did not pcap to confirm.
- **Active customer session mid-swap.** All pods were ~4h15m uptime (uniform →
  probably no active sessions at 07:30 IST Sat). Did not verify via
  `/api/v1/billing/active` (401 — would need admin JWT).
- **POS `.130` rc-agent.** Still at `e7e01ae3`. POS runs no rc-sentry so the fleet-wave

- **Admin dashboard visual verify post-deploy.** chrome-devtools-mcp sample not re-run

Header comment documents the 2026-04-12 regression history and root causes (`Get-Process` vs `Get-CimInstance` CommandLine behavior on Windows default PS) so next reader sees the prior incident. **Secondary findings (separate investigations — NOT fixed this op):** (a) HKLM Run has TWO kiosk entries pointing at different start-kiosk.bat paths (`Kiosk: C:\RacingPoint\start-kiosk.bat` + `RCKiosk: C:\RacingPoint\kiosk\start-kiosk.bat`) — canonicalization pending; (b) four overlapping schtasks (`RCKiosk`, `StartKiosk`, `StartKioskApp`, `StartKioskTemp`) similar to the Admin 5-overlap mess from 2026-04-20 06:12 IST row; (c) `FrontendWatchdog` in HKLM Run may respawn mid-deploy — untested this op; (d) `start-kiosk.bat` netstat-kill-by-port loop is race-prone under duplicate-instance startup. Direct in-process call to `crate::api::game_state::game_launch_history(State, Query)` (no router layer needed — endpoint is GET-only + unauthenticated, no middleware gap to close unlike the DPDP data-delete work earlier this session). `Query<GameHistoryQuery>` constructed via `serde_json::from_value(json!({...}))` rather than `serde_urlencoded::from_str` to avoid adding a dev-dep (first attempt failed compile with `use of unresolved module or unlinked crate serde_urlencoded` — rolled back). Test harness schema mirrors production `game_launch_events` table incl. `clean_exit_heuristic INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0` column because handler `SELECT` uses `COALESCE(clean_exit_heuristic, 0)` which requires the column to exist (COALESCE handles NULLs, not missing columns). Result: `cargo test -p racecontrol-crate --lib game_launch_history_filter_tests` → 5 passed; 0 failed; 0 ignored; 0.01s. **NOT TESTED this op:** (1) router-layer dispatch of `/api/v1/game-launch-history` — endpoint is public/unauth so no middleware gap exists; (2) the `real_crashes_only` + `limit` params already covered by absent handler tests — out of scope for this targeted filter op; (3) SQL injection via `sim_type` — `sim_type` goes through sqlx bind parameter, not string concat, so parameterized; (4) `since=` value that is not valid RFC3339 — current handler binds the string as-is to sqlite `created_at > ?` comparison; sqlite does lexicographic string comparison which only works correctly for ISO-8601 extended format; a malformed `since` produces degraded behavior (returns all or none) not a parse error; (5) admin dashboard consumer for these params — that is Part B of the backlog item and is out of scope for this op (discussed separately — needs UI-SPEC per standing rule since there is NO existing frontend consumer for `/api/v1/game-launch-history` anywhere in `web/`, `pwa/`, or `kiosk/`); (6) server .23 rebuild — test-only change, no binary redeploy required; (7) Bono VPS cloud parity — test-only change, no deploy required. Part B (admin dashboard `since=`/`sim_type=` consumer) remains open and will require its own planning turn (scope: new UI component, incremental polling pattern, possibly a feature flag) before implementation.
